#                                      3 Buggy), read by 0x001feea0 with "AppStart\CinemaCam.ini".
#                                      The file: 5 presets, "Flight Cam 1" (AngleY 180, AngleX 65,
#                                      FollowingDistance 13, FOV 85, LookAhead 20), "Flight Cam 2"
#                                      (9, 15), "Slot Cam" (120/40, 14), "Lookback Cam", "1P Stunt";
#                                      h/v/w/x variants for split screen and wide
machine_placement boot 0x0020f6e0 "%s%s\machine.txt" [Machines] NumberOfMachines; for a player whose
#                                 event vehicle kind (event +0x11c + i*0xc) is 1 it reads
#                                 "Biplane%d" (i+1): Type, Position, Direction, HasPlayer, HasAI,
#                                 MachineRacer, SetGroundHeight; else "Machine%d". free06:
#                                 [Biplane1] 2304,135,2281 dir 0,0,-1
freeride_near_machine boot 0x003a8330 the switch trigger, each frame for the HUD's player: for kinds
#                                     0..5 ("MotocrossVehicle", "Biplane", "Helicopter", "MXBuggy",
#                                     "MXMonsterTruck", "MXBaja"), unless the kind is locked in the
#                                     "Machines" unlock list, the nearest such actor to the rider;
#                                     within 30 ft and with no driver (+0x20), it goes to the HUD's
#                                     +0x6d4[player] and the kind to +0x6dc[player]
freeride_switch_prompt boot 0x003a7210 shows "Would you like to switch to the Biplane?"
#                                      (STR_SWITCH_BIPLANE via 0x00360d30, which keeps the machine
#                                      at +0x58 and kind at +0x5c) unless the rider is wrecked or
#                                      above 58.667 ft/s (40 mph); clears +0x6d4 every frame
freeride_switch_commit boot 0x003601b0 (prompt, player) the prompt's answer: machine_swap with the
#                                      prompt's machine, then the camera and a kind-numbered
#                                      message (biplane 0x13). The pause menu's "Switch To Machine:
#                                      %s" (0x0028fcf0) calls the same swap
# P103, live on free09 (PCSX2, a Monster Truck unlocked by setting read32(0x4e2778)+0x5c, the cheat
#   flag freeride_near_machine also lets through): the prompt is the Flash popup free_popup_1.fsh, in
#   layout px of 640x448 a rounded (r 10) black panel at alpha ~0x94, (181,317,280,84); the question
#   in yearlingdashbold_20, grey ~200, two centred lines inking from tops 336 and 356, squeezed to
#   0.9 of the font's width (line one 215 px), broken after "switch"; a black pill (253,385,136,33)
#   with nav_circle_off's 16 px button at (278,395) and "Accept" in yearlingdashextrabold_18 at
#   (301,398). Circle answers it: the truck actor 0xe1e210 took the human's driver 0x12939f0 and a
#   "Monster Truck Driving Controls" card showed; Cross does nothing. A first 150 ms Circle closed
#   the prompt without a swap, unexplained; a 300 ms one after a state reload swapped.
#   Journal 2026-10-22-p103.
machine_swap boot 0x00210b90 (world, controller, &current, old, new, player, ...) the old machine
#                            loses the controller (+0x14 = 0); if it is the bike its model nodes are
#                            hidden (0x0027d670 with 0 clears bit 0x40) and 0x0027b9f0 runs. No
#                            transform is written: the bike stays where it was left. The new machine
#                            gets the controller (+0x14 = 1) and the player's camera from its list
#                            entry; a biplane is unwrecked (vt +0x1c4), +0xe50 = 0, and
#                            0x00200f00(app, player, 0) runs
# C7 -- the biplane (Free06's plane, biplane.p2m2): its class, its controls as the game reads them, the
#   prop and the crash state. Read from the decompilation and proved on the capture
#   context/capture/acceptance/biplane.csv (watch/biplane.toml); journal 2026-10-20-c7-c7-biplane-capture.
# The plane is a type-1 actor in the GameManager's list (@game_manager -> 0x34 -> 0x34, nodes {item, prev,
#   next +8}); in biplane.p2m2's state it is entry 0 (actor 0xc44a40), its player record (+0x20) is the
#   human's -- the same record the human's bike (entry 7, 0xa00050) points at -- with +0x34 = 1, and
#   +0x334 = 1 while the plane is the one being flown. vtable 0x45b250 (base 0x45b050).
biplane_load            boot 0x00350a70 (1908 bytes) the load body: builds paths under "Biplane\" (string
#                                       0x4e2540), reads "%sbiplane.phy" into +0x230 through vtable +0x194
biplane_ctor            boot 0x003505f0 (940 bytes) zeroes the fields
biplane_base_dtor       boot 0x003509a0 (196 bytes) the base class's destructor (vtable 0x45b050)
biplane_phy_read        boot 0x003511f0 (3072 bytes) the .phy keys (strings 0x43ed90..0x43f2b0):
#   engine object +0x400 (MaxPower +0x4c, MaxAltitude +0x54, MinPropEfficiency +0x3c,
#   MinPropEfficiencySpeed +0x40 x 1.46666); aero object +0x404 (Chord, Span, ... +0x4c..+0xc4); tyres
#   +0x408/+0x40c/+0x410; ThrottleIncrement +0x368, ReverseThrottle +0x36c, Aileron/Elevator/Rudder
#   ControlRateLimit +0x370/+0x374/+0x378; MaxElevator +0x37c, MaxAileron +0x380, MaxRudder +0x384,
#   ElevatorRampOffRollAngle +0x38c, ElevatorOffRollAngle +0x390, MinElevator +0x394; autopilot
#   AileronDeadZone +0x398, MaxPitchRate +0x39c, MaxRollAngle +0x3a0, PitchRateFeedForwardscale +0x3a4,
#   PitchRateGain +0x3a8, PitchRateIntegratorGain +0x3ac; MinWreckSpeed +0x3b8 (x 1.46666, 73.33 in
#   the state), WreckTime +0x3c0 (5), WreckGravity +0x3c4, WreckDamping +0x3d0, WreckInertia +0x3e0
player_input_dispatch   boot 0x0025e910 (836 bytes) the player record's per-tick input: sticks at
#                                       +0x324 (x) +0x328 (y) +0x32c +0x330, the 32-entry control table at
#                                       +0x334 (stride 0x18, value at +4), and the controlled actor's
#                                       vtable +0x20 with (sticks, actor, table, pad)
biplane_input           boot 0x00351e20 (820 bytes; vtable +0x20 through the thunk 0x00355f40) the eight
#   controls, as commands looked up with pad_control_index: +0x338 throttle up = command 0xe (Cross),
#   +0x33c throttle down = 0xf (Square); +0x340 elevator = -sign(y)|y|^3 of stick y, or +1 for command 2
#   (Up) and -1 for 3 (Down) -- -1 is climb, +1 dive; +0x344 aileron = -sign(x)|x|^3, or +1 for command 0
#   (Left) and -1 for 1 (Right); +0x348 rudder = -1 for command 0x14 (L1), +1 for 0x15 (R1). Bindings as
#   biplane.p2m2's state holds them for pad 0
biplane_joystick        boot 0x00352160 (no Ghidra function; vtable +0x19c, dt in $f12) gated on +0x334;
#   rate-limits the commands into +0x354 pitch (x a roll-based scale from +0x5f8, +0x5d4 and
#   0x38c/0x390/0x394), +0x35c aileron (toward +0x344 x MaxAileron at +0x370), +0x360 rudder (toward
#   +0x348 x MaxRudder at +0x378), +0x364 nose wheel (toward +0x348); the throttle +0x34c moves by
#   ThrottleIncrement x dt, clamped to [ReverseThrottle, 1]; +0x350 the down value. Not flown: all zero
biplane_autopilot       boot 0x00352460 (no Ghidra function; vtable +0x1a0) the pitch-rate loop: integrator
#   +0x584 clamped to +-MaxElevator, elevator deflection +0x358; or +0x358 = +0x354 x MaxElevator
biplane_measure         boot 0x00352960 (1204 bytes) per tick: +0x590 on the ground (any tyre's +0x88),
#   +0x594 the last one, +0x598 speed |body+0x70|, +0x5c0 local velocity, +0x5b0 local angular rate,
#   +0x59c alpha, +0x5a0 beta, +0x5f0 euler from the body quaternion, +0x600 the drawn transform
#   (vu_quat_to_mat3; position = body+0x60 less the rotated CG offset body+0x180), +0x5d0 its forward
#   row, +0x5e0 its up row (+0x5e4 up.y); then vtable +0x1b8 and +0x1bc
#   The body is +0x160: +0x60 position, +0x70 velocity, +0x170 quaternion, +0x180 CG offset (0, 3.5,
#   -2.5), +0x1b0 angular velocity
biplane_animate         boot 0x00352ff0 (388 bytes; vtable +0x1b8) flaps from +0x354 x MaxElevator, +0x35c
#                                       and +0x360; the prop node +0x428 turned by the engine's +0x48
#                                       while the crash state is 0
biplane_wreck_step      boot 0x00353180 (1104 bytes; vtable +0x1bc) the crash state +0x3b0: 0 flying, 1
#   crashed (a contact above MinWreckSpeed, a surface that is not ignored, or a contact with up.y < 0),
#   0xd thrown (outside the area, by position_query below: velocity toward the centre +155.4 up and
#   random spins); +0x3bc its clock, +0x3b4 "the thrown plane has landed"; recovery through vtable
#   +0x1c4 once +0x3bc passes WreckTime
biplane_recover         boot 0x003536e0 (252 bytes; vtable +0x1c4) back to the start, crash state 0
biplane_set_wreck       boot 0x00354aa0 (120 bytes; vtable +0x1c0)
biplane_reset_chord     boot 0x00355df0 (332 bytes) commands 0x4c and 0x4d together (L1 and R1 on pad 0)
#   while on the ground or crashed, once (+0xe0c): biplane_recover early. biplane.p2m2's first crash
#   ends this way at 2.73 s
position_query          boot 0x001a6e50 (184 bytes) the wreck step's outside-the-area test (150.0)
biplane_engine_ctor     boot 0x003500a0 (160 bytes) vtable 0x45afd0; +0x30 points at the throttle
#                                       (plane+0x34c), +0x38 at plane+0x5d4
biplane_engine_step     boot 0x003501b0 (1084 bytes) +0x5c power = MaxPower x throttle, +0x60 prop rpm =
#   lerp(+0x64 = 500, +0x68 = 2500) by throttle, +0x6c thrust = power x 550 x altitude x efficiency /
#   max(speed, 1), +0x48 prop angle += +0x44 (217.25) x 0.1047198 x dt wrapped to +-pi, +0x70 the
#   sound's rpm with a wobble, +0x74 its time, +0x58 the body
biplane_aero_step       boot 0x0034fcb0 (1008 bytes) the aero object's step (coefficients +0x48..+0xf8)
# --- S33: the biplane's .phy loader, and where it leaves the plane -------------------------------
# (for R8: names found here, not by R8. The class is MXBiplane, "MXBiplane.cpp" at 0x0043f410.)
biplane_init boot 0x00350a70 (plane, ...) -> builds the plane: the prop models ("propblur",
#                                "propconebent"), then operator new for its parts — plane+0x400 the
#                                engine (0x78 bytes, ctor 0x003500a0), +0x404 the wing (0x100, ctor
#                                0x0034fa90), +0x408/+0x40c/+0x410 the FrontLeft/FrontRight/Rear gear
#                                (0x1d0 each, ctor 0x0034eb40, init 0x0034ecd0) — then formats
#                                "%sbiplane.phy" (0x0043ed78) with path_prefix "Biplane\" into
#                                plane+0x230, opens it and calls vtable slot +0x194 on it
biplane_phy_read boot 0x003511f0 (plane, reader) -> the base loader, vtable 0x0045b050 slot +0x194:
#                                [Mass Properties] through physics_part_read (0x0015a500) on
#                                plane+0x50 (so the body is plane+0x160, the collision record +0x170);
#                                [Engine] MaxPower +0x4c, MaxAltitude +0x54, MinPropEfficiency +0x3c,
#                                MinPropEfficiencySpeed +0x40 (x MPH) of the engine; [Aerodynamics]
#                                Chord/Span/SurfaceArea/AspectRatio/DefaultAngleOfAttack (x DEG) to
#                                biplane_wing_geometry, then CLRoundOff..CMde at wing+0x54..+0xac (23
#                                floats, CM0 at +0xa0), CmBreakAngleOfAttack +0x4c (x DEG), CMstall,
#                                Cnbeta, Cnp, Cnr, Cndr, Cnda at +0xb0..+0xc4; MaxElevator +0x37c,
#                                MaxAileron +0x380, MaxRudder +0x384, ElevatorRampOffRollAngle +0x38c,
#                                ElevatorOffRollAngle +0x390 (all x DEG), MinElevator +0x394 (as read)
#                                on the plane; each Tire section to the three gear setters below;
#                                [Autopilot] AileronDeadZone +0x398, MaxPitchRate +0x39c, MaxRollAngle
#                                +0x3a0 (x DEG), PitchRateFeedForwardscale +0x3a4, PitchRateGain
#                                +0x3a8, PitchRateIntegratorGain +0x3ac; [Joystick] ThrottleIncrement
#                                +0x368, ReverseThrottle +0x36c, Aileron/Elevator/RudderControlRateLimit
#                                +0x370/+0x374/+0x378; [Wrecks] WreckTime +0x3c0, MinWreckSpeed +0x3b8
#                                (x MPH), WreckGravity +0x3c4, WreckDamping +0x3d0, WreckInertia +0x3e0
#                                (quadwords). Then copies the body's inertia (body+0x110) to
#                                plane+0x3f0. Every float getter is ini_float_default: 0, except the
#                                gear's first six (1.0)
mxbiplane_phy_read boot 0x00355210 (plane, reader) -> MXBiplane's override, vtable 0x0045b250 slot
#                                +0x194: biplane_phy_read, then [Animation] PelvisBoxPosition +0xe70,
#                                PelvisBoxSize +0xe80
biplane_wing_geometry boot 0x0034fc40 (chord, span, area, ratio, aoa in f12..f16, wing) -> +0x38..+0x48;
#                                a zero chord with a positive ratio becomes span / ratio
biplane_gear_springs boot 0x0034ee10 (StrutCompression, MaxDecompressionRate, SpringCoefficient,
#                                Bounce-, ReboundDampingRatio in f12..f16, gear) -> +0x3c, +0x40, the
#                                rate |coef * mass * 32.2| at +0x44 and ratio * 2 * sqrt(mass * rate) at
#                                +0x48/+0x4c; the mass is the body's (gear+0x12c -> +0xc)
biplane_gear_tire boot 0x0034ee90 (TireRadius, SideForce, BrakeFriction, MaxBrakingForce,
#                                SteeringMoment in f12..f16, gear) -> +0x150, 1/r at +0x154 when r > 0,
#                                +0x15c, +0x158, +0x160, +0x164. The front legs get a literal 0 moment
biplane_gear_contact boot 0x0034eee0 (gear) -> +0xa0 = BodyTireCenter (+0x60) one radius lower; the
#                                running gear rewrites it as the strut works
# physics_part_read (0x0015a500), shared with Wreckbike.phy, leaves on the plane: GravityScale
# part+0x140, CollisionEnabled +0x54, DetectCollisionsWhenDisabled +0x6c, ResetOffEdgeOfWorld +0x70,
# ResetTime..BodyAngularSpeedImpulseThreshold +0x1c0..+0x1d8, gravity +0xf0; the body gets the mass
# at +0xc (1/m at +0x10, inertia +0x110). Collision record: mass +0x64, CofG offset +0x90, high-speed
# flag +0x3c, ray offset +0xb0, scale/distance +0xc0/+0xc4, MaxPenetration/SpringStiffness/
# SpringDamping +0x118/+0x11c/+0x120, rate/damping +0x124/+0x128, moment scales +0x150/+0x160/+0x170,
# FrictionZone1/BodyFriction +0x190/+0x194, the sixteen surface frictions +0x198.
# In the biplane recording's state (biplane.p2m2_SaveState.p2s) the plane is at 0x00c44a40.
# AeroSurfaceArea (0x004437d0) is NOT a biplane key: it is read by 0x00394450, another vehicle's
# loader (clutch, NumberOfWings, "Wing %d") — which vehicle is R8's to say.
#
# V21 — the biplane, drawn. Names for R8, found while drawing it; none of these is ported as code, the
# port is crates/game-render/src/biplane.rs (model, cones, seat, emitter list) and the entry is
# research/journal/2026-10-20-v21-v21-the-biplane-drawn. "Biplane.cpp" is the string at 0x0043ed48,

vehicle_spawn       boot 0x0020eb10 (…, kind, livery, …) kind 1 is the biplane: model by livery 0 "biplane"

#                                  copies the spawn to +0xe10..+0xe3c and adds the actor to the manager

biplane_ctor        boot 0x003505f0 the base Biplane constructor

#                                  to +0x900 (the pilot's seat offset, short of the measured 3.5 in y)

biplane_load        boot 0x00350a70 reads "%sbiplane.phy" (0x0043ed78); loads propconeblur (0x0043ed58)

biplane_pose_nodes  boot 0x00352ff0 per frame: the four wing flaps +0x418..+0x424 turned by the aileron

# RAM on the biplane recording's states: the plane actor is type 1; its +0x180 is not a world
# transform, *(plane+0x18c) is its model's root node and node+0x80 the world matrix (rows x, y, z,
# position — the bike's convention). The pilot's root node sat at (0, 3.5, -3.0) in the plane's space,
# with the plane's rotation, on all four states read (00, 12, 36, 52).

# S34 — the biplane's own flight (crates/game-sim/src/biplane.rs, proofs in tests/biplane.rs). The
# names below repeat R8's for the same addresses (R8 is on its own branch; first match wins). Offsets
# are MXBiplane's. The live plane in context/states/biplane.p2m2_SaveState is at 0x00c44a40.
boot_gp boot 0x00467770 the value crt0 puts in $gp (0x100148..0x100170); anything gp-relative needs it
biplane_controls boot 0x00352160 (self, dt in f12): see R8. Pad +0x338 forward, +0x33c back, +0x340
#                                elevator, +0x344 aileron, +0x348 rudder; the elevator fade reads
#                                |+0x5d4| (forward axis y) < 0.8 and |+0x5f8| (roll)
biplane_autopilot boot 0x00352460 (self, dt in f12): turn feed-forward from the curve table and
#                                 *(+0x160)+0x1b0 (the body's body-frame pitch rate), +0x5c8 airspeed
biplane_engine_step boot 0x003501b0 (engine at +0x400, dt in f12): +0x30 -> throttle, +0x38 -> +0x5d4,
#                                   +0x58 the body (+0x60 position, +0x70 velocity); writes +0x5c
#                                   power, +0x60 rpm, +0x6c thrust, +0x48 prop angle, +0x70 note,
#                                   +0x74 clock; thrust (0,0,T,1) to the body's vt +0x58
biplane_aero_forces boot 0x0034fcb0 (aero at +0x404): +0xc8.. pointers to +0x5c8 speed, +0x59c alpha,
#                                   +0x5a0 beta, +0x358/+0x35c/+0x360 the surfaces, +0xe0 the body
#                                   rates (body+0x1b0), +0xe4 the body; writes CL..CM at +0xe8..+0xf8;
#                                   force to vt +0x58, moment to vt +0x5c. +0x30 nonzero: the yaw
#                                   moment's slow terms use the true speed, else max(V, [0x00421308])
body_force_local boot 0x001f7a10 (body, vec4*) the force rotated by body+0x170 into the world, then
#                                vt +0x38 adds it
body_moment_local boot 0x001f3360 (body, vec4*) body+0x190 += moment, then its +0xd0 listeners
dynamics_world_step boot 0x001f29a0 (world, dt in f12): preforce listeners (+0x98 list) with dt; n =
#                                   (int)(dt * world+4) + 1 substeps (world+4 = 115.0 in the state);
#                                   each: force listeners (+0x68) with dt/n, every body's vt +0x44
#                                   (integrate) and +0x30, the constraints (+0x80) world+0 times;
#                                   then the post listeners (+0xb0). World node 0x0060b200, step at
#                                   +0x78, called from 0x0026f800 after the collision clear/collide

# --- S36: the biplane's crash, read for the port (crates/game-sim/src/biplane.rs) and proved against
#     the biplane recording's playback (context/capture/s36/biplane-crash.csv, MXBiplane at 0xc44a40) ---
biplane_wreck_check_edge boot 0x00353180 the map-edge half: terrain extents (+0x3b4)+0x530*256*+0x538 and
#                                       +0x534*256*+0x53c (query 0x001a6e50, margin 150 a side);
#                                       target (W/2, pos.y, D/2, 1), dir normalised by 1/sqrt(mula/madda/
#                                       madd), k = 2*sqrt(14.906832 0x416e8262), speed = min(2073.6
#                                       0x45019999, W/2)/k, lift = 16.1*k + 240/k (0x4180cccd), vel = dir*speed
#                                       + lift on y, +0x194 *= 0.5, spin = (0.5, 0.4 0x3ecccccd, 0.7
#                                       0x3f333333) * three rand_raw units in x, y, z order, then wreck(13).
#                                       The same call goes on into the contact half; the edge wreck's
#                                       clock counts on its first tick, the contact wreck's does not
biplane_vehicle_flag boot 0x0043ed38 the string "VEHICLE": the surface flag (0x2000 in surface +0x84) the
#                                    speed rule spares; the plane keeps the mask at +0x580
biplane_measure_order boot 0x00353180 runs before +0x598 airspeed and +0x5e4 up.y are recomputed: the
#                                     test reads the previous tick's. Captured entries 11664 and 12498
#                                     (prev airspeed 101.38 and 137.23, both above 73.333)
biplane_gravity_bits boot 0x003535d0 neg.s / mul.s / mul.s: 32.2 (0x4200cccd) * (-scale * mass); the
#                                    body's mass (body +0xc) is 0x423a55dc = 1500 * 0.0310559 chopped,
#                                    not 1500 / 32.2 (div.s gives 0x423a55dd). Flying -1499.9999,
#                                    wrecked -2249.9998, both bit-equal in the capture
mxbiplane_respawn_arm boot 0x00355c50 the racer interface's slot (vtable 0x0045b558): (+0xd60)+0x2c == 1
#                                     resets at once, else +0xe50 = 10.0. Never armed in the freeride
#                                     capture
biplane_respawn_captured boot 0x003536e0 the waited-out wreck ends 151 ticks after entry (clock > 5.0 at
#                                        dt 0.03333); the body is at the spawn (2304, 138.614059, 2281)
#                                        facing (0,0,-1), vel and spin exactly 0, gravity back, throttle 0,
#                                        grounded the next tick. The chord's wreck ended 83 ticks in
#                                        (11747), the recording's L1+R1 held from field 1550

# S35 — the biplane's pilot and moving parts. Port: crates/game-sim/src/biplane_anim.rs (plus
# Library::add in vehicle_anim.rs); proofs: crates/game-sim/tests/biplane_anim.rs against
# context/capture/biplane/pilot.csv (watch/biplane-pilot.toml, journal 2026-10-20-s35-s35-the-biplane-moves).
biplane_pilot_request boot 0x00355000 the fourth step of MXBiplane's measure (0x00355290 = 0x00352960,
#                                     0x00354e00, this, 0x00355170), gated on (+0x8 & +0x694). Writes the
#                                     request block at +0x690 (built by 0x002450c0 in the ctor 0x00353d10):
#                                     +0x698 at_base = 0, +0x69c held = +0x588, +0x780 offset ptr =
#                                     &PelvisBoxPosition (+0xe70), +0x784 = &PelvisBoxSize (+0xe80), +0x788
#                                     airborne = !+0x590, +0x800 phase target = 0.5*(1 - +0x344), +0x804
#                                     mix target = 0.5*(1 + +0x340) (sub.s/add.s then mul.s, 0.5 first),
#                                     +0x808 band = +0x598, +0x810 base = body +0x50, +0x820 rotation = body
#                                     +0x150, +0x83c = +0x178, and the hand/foot dummies. Never written:
#                                     wrecked +0x794, gesture +0x830 (24), progress +0x834, seq +0x838,
#                                     cut +0x7d0, settle +0x7d4, commanded +0x7f0 -- the ctor's values.
#                                     The step reads the offset through the pointer (0x0024934c lw 0xf0).
body_mount boot 0x0024ea70 (body, mask, request) a body vtable slot (0x00454544, 0x00458fe4), not a
#                          Ghidra function: returns if +0x488 is set or (mask & +0x484) == 0; else +0x488
#                          = request, vt +0x140, +0x9f0 = -1 then the FIRST record i with key ==
#                          **request (the owner's type word), 0x001b7020(+0xa74, req+0x180, req+0x120),
#                          +0xa74 +0x240 = request.
#   rider_machine_clips (0x00248150): bit 0 of +0x9f8 gates it (the list ctor 0x0024ed90 clears it, the
#   setter is not located; every body on the biplane state has it set); a key already present is
#   skipped; the record is appended before its clips are looked up; per name load (0x00142430,
#   0x001445b0) then bind (0x001426a0, 0x00146370), a first; an empty name leaves 0.
biplane_reset_latch boot 0x00352910 the frame step's two lines: if (+0x588 && +0x58c) +0x588 = 0;
#                                   +0x58c = +0x588. The reset 0x00351df0 sets +0x588 = 1.
# Tick order the capture shows (1717 ticks): reset -> measure (request from the previous tick's sticks)
# -> latch, surfaces -> wreck test; the pilot's body eases its lags (+0x630 -> mix, +0x634 -> phase)
# towards this tick's block. The pilot's body is actor-list entry 7's actor - 0x400 on biplane.p2m2's
# state (0x009ffc50, request 0x00c450d0 = plane 0x00c44a40 + 0x690, one record {1, 0x0195c4f0,
# 0x00835640}); the plane's type word is 1. Axes of the surface step: 0x00426da0 (1,0,0), 0x00426db0
# (0,1,0), 0x00426dc0 (0,0,1); bone names 0x0043f2c0..0x0043f330; 0x001b2e50 replaces the rotation and
# keeps the position, 0x001b2c20 turns the restored rest matrix about the kept hinge (+0x540..+0x570)
# on the left (row-vector order).

# S38 — the rigid body the plane flies on, the world step and the measure (journal
# research/journal/2026-10-20-s38-the-biplanes-body). Body vtable 0x00451f10: +0x30 end substep,
# +0x38 add force, +0x44 integrate, +0x58 force local, +0x5c moment local, +0x64 -> +0x38.
body_integrate_linear boot 0x001f20d0 (body, h in f12): +0x50 = +0x60; unless +0x10 inv mass == 0.0,
#                                     +0xf0 = (h, 0.5*h*h, inv mass), a = +0x90 * inv mass, +0x60 +=
#                                     a h^2/2 + v h, +0x70 = a h + +0x20 * v, +0xa0 = a (w stale)
body_integrate boot 0x001f2340 (body, h in f12) vt +0x44: linear, +0x150 = +0x170; unless +0x120 ==
#                              (0,0,0,1) bitwise: second-order quaternion step from +0x1b0 rates and
#                              +0x120 * +0x190, vrsqrt normalise, rates += alpha h with +0x130 damping
body_end_substep boot 0x001f77c0 (body) vt +0x30: +0xb0/+0x1d0 keep the larger |+0xc0|/|+0x1e0| per
#                                axis; +0x80 = +0x90, +0x1a0 = +0x190; +0x90 +0xc0 +0x190 +0x1e0 cleared
body_add_force boot 0x0015afc0 (body, vec4*) vt +0x38: +0x90.xyz += v, then the +0xd0 listeners
body_wrapper_force boot 0x0015af40 (wrapper, h) force listener: if the body is enabled, vt +0x64 with
#                                  wrapper+0xf0 (the plane's gravity), then wrapper+0x120's vt +0x80
#                                  with 1 (collision response 0x00270dc0), wrapper+0x1b0 = 0
body_wrapper_preforce boot 0x0015b050 (wrapper, dt): body+0xb0 = (0,0,0,1)
body_wrapper_post boot 0x0015b070 (wrapper, dt): if enabled, origin = body+0x50 - turn(+0x150, +0x180)
#                                 to transform_set_pose (wrapper+0x13c, 0, body+0x150, &origin)
#                                 when actor vt +0x10c says so, then actor vt +0xd8, transform_touch if
#                                 wrapper+0x74, vt +0x110; wrapper+0x78 = 0
dynamics_world boot 0x0060b278 the world dynamics_world_step runs (embedded at world node +0x78): in
#                              the save state iterations 1, rate 115.0, 3 bodies, 3 force, 0
#                              constraints, 3 preforce, 3 post listeners
mxbiplane_post boot 0x00355290 (plane, dt) the post listener: biplane_measure, then 0x00354e00,
#                              0x00355000, 0x00355170 (the MX record/rider copies)
touchdown_sound boot 0x001b30e0 (out, +0x18c, body+0xb0 shock peak) then 0x003dd0a0 (+0x67c, 0, out)
transform_set_pose boot 0x001b21d0 (transform, 0, quat*, origin*) a sibling of orientation_set; the
#                                  plane's pose goes through it after every world step
sound_trigger boot 0x003dd0a0 (sound object, 0, position*) what the plane's touchdown calls after
#                             touchdown_sound

# S39 — the biplane's wheels and its crash test (crates/game-sim/src/biplane.rs, proofs in
# tests/biplane.rs). A tire is 0x1d0 bytes (ctor 0x0034eb40, init 0x0034ecd0); the plane's three at
# +0x408/+0x40c/+0x410. Tire fields: +0x30 -> brake (front: plane+0x350), +0x34 -> steer (tail:
# plane+0x364), +0x3c StrutCompression, +0x40 MaxDecompressionRate, +0x44 SpringCoefficient, +0x48/+0x4c
# damping closing/opening, +0x50 hub-to-bottom (plane frame), +0x60 BodyTireCenter, +0x80 ground at the
# bottom, +0x84 held contact (only ever cleared), +0x88 touching, +0x8c compression, +0x90 last,
# +0x94 spring force, +0xa0 bottom, +0xb0 the ground query's record, +0xc0 ground point, +0xd0 its
# normal, +0xe0 contact, +0xf0 hub (world), +0x100 bottom (world), +0x110 contact velocity, +0x120
# wheel angle, +0x124 spin, +0x128 plane, +0x12c body, +0x134 world, +0x140 last normal, +0x150
# TireRadius, +0x154 1/radius, +0x158 MaxBrakingForce, +0x15c SideForce, +0x160 BrakeFriction, +0x164
# SteeringMoment, +0x170 slip (tire frame), +0x180 friction (tire frame), +0x190 the tire frame.
biplane_tire_preforce boot 0x0034ef80 (tire) +0x80 = +0x84 = 0
biplane_tire_probe boot 0x0034ef90 (tire): the bottom of the tire one radius toward the last normal
#                              (sideways part dropped), through the plane's frame +0x600, to
#                              ground_query; +0x80 set unless the ground is below the bottom
biplane_tire_force boot 0x0034f150 (tire, dt in f12): biplane_tire_strut then biplane_tire_friction
biplane_tire_strut boot 0x0034f190 (tire, dt in f12): compression along hub-to-bottom, faded by the
#                              slope in the body's frame (floor [0x00421300] = 0.5), clamped (the
#                              push-out reads the clamped value: always 0), spring/damper, force at
#                              the contact through the body's vt +0x68; decompression rate limit
biplane_tire_friction boot 0x0034f7e0 (tire, dt in f12): side force, braking, tail wheel moment
#                              (vt +0x5c), all x clamp(0.1 x speed, -1, 1); force through the tire
#                              frame at the contact (vt +0x68); spin = slip.z / radius, angle wrapped
biplane_tire_frame boot 0x0034f9d0 (tire) mat4_basis(+0x190, origin, plane forward row +0x620, normal,
#                              1, 0); +0x170 = the contact velocity into it
biplane_collision_force boot 0x0015af40 (plane+0x50): unless the body's first word is 0: gravity
#                              (+0xf0, plane+0x140) through the body's vt +0x64, the collision object
#                              (+0x120, plane+0x170) vtable +0x80 with 1, then +0x1b0 = 0
body_push boot 0x0015afb0 (body, vec4*) vt +0x64: tail call of vt +0x38
body_add_velocity boot 0x001f7ec0 (body, vec4*) body+0x70 and body+0xc0 += v.xyz, listeners; vt +0x3c
body_force_at boot 0x001f7a70 (body, force*, point*, mask*) moment = q-rotated (point - pos) x force,
#                              masked, through vt +0x5c, then the force through vt +0x38; vt +0x68
collision_resolve boot 0x00270dc0 (collision, 1) the collision object's contact resolution (vtable
#                              0x00454a00 +0x80): ported in S42 as contact::resolve
# S42 — the resolution, per contact in the list: the relative velocity, then (normal speed under -10)
# the impulse when its second argument is set and the contact is of kind 0, or (otherwise) the spring;
# then the friction always, then the damping when +0x50 is set and the record is on a body (+0xf4).
collision_relative_velocity boot 0x00270f60 (record, entry) -> +0xa0 the centre into the world, the
#                              entry's arms (+0x50, +0x60), +0x1e0 the relative velocity, +0x1d8 along
#                              the normal; the other side a body (+0xf4), a chassis (+0xf0, transform
#                              +0xf8) or the entry's mover (+0x78: vt +0xbc spin, +0xb8 velocity,
#                              transform +0x254, origin +0x190)
collision_spring boot 0x00271590 (record, entry) the damped spring at the anchor, the actor's tuning
#                              (+0x13c) or the scenery's (+0x124), depth capped at +0x118
collision_impulse boot 0x002719b0 (record, entry) the impulse along the normal, restitution +0x12c or
#                              +0x144, angular terms when +0x148/+0x14c
collision_friction boot 0x002722d0 (record, entry) weight 32.2 x mass x min(0.1 |slide|, 1) x mu
#                              against the slide; mu +0x190/+0x194 or the surface's at +0x198
collision_damp boot 0x002724c0 (record, entry) lowers body damping (vt +0x1c) and angular damping
#                              (vt +0x54) towards +0x58/+0x60 along the normal and its moment axis
collision_apply boot 0x00272880 (record, force, point, mask) the masked moment (vt +0x5c), then the
#                              force (vt +0x64)
body_add_rates boot 0x001f7bc0 (body, vec4*) body+0x1b0 and +0x1e0 += v.xyz; vt +0x70
body_set_damping boot 0x0015aba0 (body, vec4*) body+0x20 = v, the whole quad; vt +0x1c
body_set_ang_damping boot 0x0015ab80 (body, vec4*) body+0x130 = v, the whole quad; vt +0x54
# transform_unrotate (0x001b30e0) transposes in the integer registers; its third column is a 64-bit
# move over the third row, which keeps that row's z and w, so the transpose is whole.
# S43 — the contact search (collision_record_collide, vt +0x7c), ported as contact_search::collide
terrain_point_query boot 0x001dce80 (terrain, buf) buf+0 point in, out: y, buf+0x10 normal (weighted,
#                              normalised), buf+0x20 four surface bytes, buf+0x30 corner weights; cell
#                              step 2 when terrain+0x8a8; diagonal by terrain+0x760==1 or cell parity;
#                              a miss leaves y/weights, writes normal (0,1,0,1) and surfaces 0
terrain_cell_read boot 0x001ddaf0 (tree, ix, iz, heights, normals, surfaces, step) -> 0 on a miss;
#                              behind a 10-entry cache of 0x70 at 0x0049f540 keyed ix<<16|iz only
#                              (not by terrain or step); heights scaled by terrain+0x80 on a miss
collision_record_sweep_back boot 0x00270390 (record, dt) the sweep from pos - vel*lead*dt to the
#                              turned offset; refuses when both sides are moving (>1) and their
#                              directions' dot > +0xc8 facing (lead +0xc0, push +0xc4); else body+0x60 =
#                              point + push*out; needs +0x3c and the sweep probe +0x1f4
# The ground result collide builds on its stack is the object at 0x00130950 (vtable 0x0044daf0:
# +8 set, +0x10 argmax surface strict from 0.0, +0x14 depth = 0 always, +0x18 point, +0x1c normal);
# depth 0 is why no water reaches the contacts here. orientation_transform_point adds the origin.
# transform_set_position on a parentless node writes local +0x70 and only marks dirt (bit0 of +0x110,
# children via +0x104); the world copy +0x80.. is rebuilt later, not in the search.
# S44 — the nearby search (collide_against_search, above), ported as world_search::search_nearby.
# A collidable's flag byte +0x116: 0x01 never searches placed groups, 0x04 can be struck, 0x08 the
# gather keeps it only within its bound, 0x10 searched this frame, 0x20 the last search hit. +2 is the
# class (2 a collidable, 3 a placed group), +0x68 the shape kind (0 mesh, 1 hierarchy, 2 rays,
# 3 spheres). The plane's ray probe has class vtable (+0x38) 0x00451a90, its sweep probe 0x00451910;
# the save state's candidates are meshes (0x00451b50) and hierarchies (0x00451c10).
collision_pair_filter boot 0x00119d70 (a, b, nearby) -> may a collide with b: not itself, b's 0x04,
#                              b+0x31 bit0 when nearby, a+0xcc & b+0x84, then the object lists
#                              (+0xd0 mode, +0xd4/+0xd8; +0xdc mode, +0xe0/+0xe4: mode 1 excludes,
#                              mode 0 is the only ones) a's, b's, a's, b's, then the category lists
#                              (+0xe8 mode, +0xec/+0xf0: mode 1 excludes, else one must match and
#                              an empty list matches nothing) a's about b+0x84, b's about a+0x84
collidable_listener_at boot 0x0011cb80 (list, i) -> list+8's i-th word; a collidable's listeners are
#                              the list at +0xf4 (count +0xf4, array +0xfc)
group_part_count boot 0x00129460 (group) -> the parts of a placed group's model: the world manager
#                              (word at 0x0046a5f8) +0x94's model table indexed by group+0xe, +0x194
group_part boot 0x00129490 (group, i) -> part i made ready as a collidable, or 0
ray_probe_clear boot 0x001eb280 ray probe class vt +0x90: lowers +0x116 0x20, empties the result +0x6c
ray_probe_pair boot 0x001eb520 ray probe class vt +0x94: stashes probe+0x174, tail-calls
#                              ray_collide_pair
ray_probe_prepare boot 0x001ebd20 ray probe class vt +0xb0
sphere_probe_clear boot 0x001e88d0 sphere (sweep) probe class vt +0x90
sphere_probe_pair boot 0x001e8d40 sphere probe class vt +0x94: stashes probe+0x164, tail-calls
#                              sphere_collide_pair
sphere_probe_prepare boot 0x001e93e0 sphere probe class vt +0xb0
gather_resort boot 0x002189d0 collision_search_gather's path when the centre has not moved past the bound
collidable_distance boot 0x0011c120 (0, object, point) the gather's per-candidate distance
world_tree_query_init boot 0x001bb390 the broad phase's tree query, on a stack buffer
world_tree_query_begin boot 0x001bb3e0 (query, tree, kind, centre, half, lo out, hi out, 1)

world_tree_query_end boot 0x001bb890

# S95 — the biplane strikes the ride's bikes (flight_search::world_among / Scene::among, flight.rs
#   frame_among, lib.rs Sim::step; proofs in crates/game-sim/tests/flight_bikes.rs over biplane.p2m2).
#   bike_struck_listener is `sw a2, 0x7d8(a0)`: search_nearby (0x0011a9d0) calls the struck collidable's
#   listeners' vt +0xc with (listener, struck, striker probe), and the bike keeps the striker at +0x7d8.
#   bike_wreck_test's last clause: +0x7d8 != 0 and (striker +0x84 & bike +0x398) != 0 -> vt +0xfc(bike, 5)
#   (Wreck::Flat); +0x7d8 is cleared after, on both exits. +0x398 is 0x100000 on every bike in the state,
#   and the plane's probes' category is 8, so a plane never wrecks a bike through this.
#   A bike collidable (three among the plane's 34: found[23..26]): class 2, shape kind 0 (mesh), flags
#   0x14, +0x31 bit 0, category 0x9a048, collides_with 0xffffffff, categories mode 1 empty, one listener
#   at bike +0x80 (vtable 0x00454fc0); +0x78 the bike, +0x7c the chassis, +0x80 the bike's collision
#   record; +0x120 the chassis node's world transform; +0x234 the mesh, bike\col\bikef.col (900 bytes,
#   12 triangles, identity transform; in every freeNN_ss_2.res and Bike\*.res). +0x60 radius 4.30349 =
#   |root half extent|; +0x40 the root box's centre through +0x120 (both bit-exact in the test).
#   The moving list: ray probe, sweep probe, then the bikes (the wrecked one's slot freed, the bike still
#   a candidate from an earlier gather). The flying player's own bike's collidable is at space 0 (out of
#   the world), flags 0x94.
# S48 — the biplane searches the world (crates/game-sim/src/flight_search.rs, flight.rs; proofs in
# tests/flight_collision.rs over biplane.p2m2_SaveState). The hit listener's this is plane+0x50 (the

# word is 0x40, which the meshes' category 0x48 meets.
plane_body_wake boot 0x0015b790 (body) vt +0x124: only when the word +0x110 points at is 0 (asleep)
#                              re-seats the +0x138 frames and re-enables it. The port takes a riding
#                              plane's body as awake, so its hit listener does nothing (that word was
#                              not read off the state)
collision_mesh_load boot 0x001eded0 a .col's meshes: each facet normal packs to the low 16 bits of
#                              (n + 768.0)'s float bits, as i16 (flight_search::pack_normal)
# The plane's collidable off the state: category 0x42058, actors 8, collides-with 0xffffffff; its
# probes: rays from Biplane\col\biplanem.col (category 0x8, flags 0x12), the sweep (flags 0x13).

# S96 — the plane's candidate order (crates/game-sim/src/broad_build.rs, flight_search.rs world/search;
# proofs in tests/flight_candidates.rs over biplane.p2m2_SaveState and the disc). How it was found is in
# research/journal/2026-10-21-s96-the-planes-candidate-order.
broad_tree_ctor boot 0x00195d00 the static quadtree over a ground-plane box: scale = 32768 /
#                              (float)(int) the longer extent; levels halve from 16384 cells until
#                              (int)(least * scale) >= the level's size, or 12 levels; max = levels - 1.
#                              Tree +0x30/+0x38 lo x/z, +0x34/+0x3c hi x/z, +0x48/+0x4c extent, +0x58 the
#                              smallest node's side in cells, +0x5c scale, +0x94 root; flags +0x14 (0x30033
#                              in the state: bit 1 = its queries report their box)
world_tree_make boot 0x001c9d80 called from the level build 0x001a4540 with least-node width 96.0 over
#                              the terrain field's box (free06: 0..6912 both ways, 6 levels, cell 256)
broad_tree_key boot 0x00196110 a box's key: clamped to lo..hi, 0 when x1 < 0, !(x0 <= extent.x), z1 < 0 or
#                              !(z0 <= extent.z); the first level the box does not fit both ways (capped
#                              at max), cells masked to it, one level up if a masked span still exceeds
#                              the side; corner bits 0x10 always, 0x20 z spills, 0x40 x spills, 0x80 both;
#                              x0/z0 clamped to 0x7fff; key = level | corners | z0 << 5 | x0 << 17
broad_tree_insert boot 0x00196320 per corner bit, down level+1 steps from bit 0x4000, child 2*xbit + zbit,
#                              nodes made where missing (node +0 children array of 4, +4 chunk chain)
broad_chunk_add boot 0x00196c20 the first empty slot of the node's chunks (chunk +0 next, +4..+0x10 four
#                              slots), else a new chunk appended at the end
mesh_collidable_bounds boot 0x001edce0 a mesh collidable's box: the matrix at +0x120, the root node of the
#                              mesh at +0x234 (+0xc centre, +0x18 half); centre = T + r0*c.x + r1*c.y +
#                              r2*c.z on the VU ACC (adda T, madda r0, madda r1, madd r2), half = |r0*h.x|
#                              + |r1*h.y| + |r2*h.z| (adda, maddw by 1); both w 1. The centre is +0x40,
#                              which is what the gather measures from (not the FPU transform_point's)
scene_collision_read boot 0x001aa760 a .scn collision placement: Position/Look/Up through ini_quadword
#                              (strtok, strtod, the truncating double_to_float 0x00418700 per lane) and
#                              mat4_basis(out, pos, look, up, orthogonalise 0, keep_forward 1). The port's
#                              track.rs had rounded to nearest and world.rs orthogonalised: 345 of free06's
#                              353 meshes were a bit out before S96
# Adding to the world: 0x00119c60 -> 0x001adb70, which calls 0x001ae000 (into the table's other/sub
# tables) only when its 4th argument is set; the plane's table has no sub tables in the state (not ported).
# The state's world (the ray probe's space +4 is its table, table +0x34 the tree, +0x38 the moving list:
# +0x30 block size 1024, +0x34 blocks 1, +0x38 last 122, +0x3c the block array; a free slot holds a link
# with bit 1 set): 353 scene meshes, category 0x220, flags 0x0c (some 0x1c/0x4c/0x8c: 0x10/0x20 a search's
# marks, 0x40/0x80 unread by the search), +0x31 bit 0 set, mask 0xffffffff, empty object and category lists
# (mode 1), class 2, in 769 node entries over 416 nodes. Class-3 placed groups (bushes, models of 0 parts)
# are in the tree too and take gather capacity only.
# The plane's search (record +0x10c): least 10, extra 30, capacity 128, no_groups 0. Its 34 candidates are
# all moving-list objects in list order: 23 toys (category 0x48), 3 bikes (one has left the world, its slot
# a free link now), 8 machine race gates (category 0x200020; gate init 0x003b0a70, the machine race
# 0x003a2e90). plane_refresh_search's own caller was not found. (S99: found, the block below.)
# S99 — when the plane's search refills (crates/game-sim/src/flight.rs refresh, flight_search.rs refresh;
# proof in tests/flight_candidates.rs over a second state, biplane_later.p2s). How it was found is in
# research/journal/2026-10-22-s99-when-the-planes-search-refills.
# Callers of vt +0x110 on the plane's collision owner: body_wrapper_post 0x0015b070 (call 0x0015b180, its
# last step before +0x78 = 0, so only when the body is enabled), the asleep check 0x0015b2a0 (interface
# thunk 0x0015c2b0 at owner+0x50, dt in f12: refreshes only while the body sleeps, word at *(+0x110) == 0,
# and +0x6c is set) and the wake 0x0015b790 (vt +0x124). Five single-slot interface vptrs sit at
# owner+0x40..+0x50 (vtables 0x0044e7e0/7ec/7f8/808/814, this-adjusting thunks 0x0015c260..0x0015c2b0).
probe_object_moved boot 0x00119be0 (probe) probe vt +0xa8 -- for the ray probe class (vtable 0x00451a90)
#                              0x001ec750: the node's (+0x164) world matrix via 0x001b2a50, then vt +0xac
#                              recomputes the reach (+0x40/+0x60) -- then 0x00119d00 (vt +0xa4 box, and
#                              0x001ab310 moves it in its table)
# The clock, from a live trace (call hooks at the entries, biplane state, ticks 10993..11004 alike): per
# 30 Hz tick, every collision record's contact search first (collision_record_collide 0x002708a0 from
# 0x0026f874; the plane's record first, collide_against_search on its sweep, then its ray probe), then
# dynamics_world_step 0x001f29a0 (world 0x0060b278, from 0x0026f8dc) and its post listeners: the plane's
# body_wrapper_post (from biplane_measure 0x00352980, its first step) refreshes and gathers its search --
# once a tick, around the ray probe's reach at the stepped pose -- then the two driving toys' posts (from
# 0x001f2c00) refresh their own, then other gathers and the bike world (0x0030f7ec, world 0x00a83830).
# So S96's five stale distances are the objects that move after the plane's gather in the same tick (the
# two toys in their posts, the bikes in their world) -- proven bit for bit with a state saved holding the
# centres copied at the plane's refresh.
# Recovery does not gather: biplane_unwreck 0x003536e0 -> plane vt +0x198 (mxbiplane_reset 0x00355b00) ->
# 0x00351df0 -> 0x0015a030 takes the probes out and puts them back (0x001ab1d0, 0x00119c60) but does not
# call the refresh. The recovery comes after the body post (the crash step's, later in the post listener
# as the port has it) or before the frame (the reset chord): either way the next search uses the list
# gathered before the put-back (read from the code, not seen live: no recovery in the traced ticks).

# S47 — the sweep probe's narrow phase (crates/game-sim/src/sweep_probe.rs, proofs in
# tests/sweep_probe.rs). sphere_probe_clear: count = min(+0x120, 16); per segment the ends +0x128
# (32 bytes a segment) through their nodes +0x124 (two pointers a segment; the node's world matrix is
# at node+0x80..+0xb0, a null node leaves the end as it is), world origin +0x138, unit direction +0x13c
# (the zero quad's xyz when the length is not > 0), length +0x134; the box of all ends gives the centre
# +0x150 (copied to +0x40) and radius +0x160 (copied to +0x60). It also lowers bit 0x20 of +0x116,
# zeroes the globals 0x00468a68 and 0x00468a60 (no other xrefs found), stores the answer +0x6c in the
# global 0x00468258 (through 0x0011c110, which only does that) and resets the answer: +0/+0x10 the
# origin quad, +0xb0 = 1, +0x8c = 0. sphere_probe_pair stores +0x164 in the global 0x00468250 and
# tail-calls sphere_collide_pair; that global and a mesh descriptor's +4 (per-vertex normals) switch
# mesh_sweep_level to smoothed normals (0x0010f2a0, 0x0010ea00, 0x0010db20, chosen at
# 0x0010d418..0x0010d4cc). The plane's +0x164 is 0. Local forms: origins +0x12c, directions +0x130,
# centre +0x140; the lengths are not rescaled. A hierarchy's part is struck only for segments whose
# owner word is still 0 (then set to 1); a mesh sets no owner. +0x9c is the collidable struck.
# sphere_probe_prepare is misnamed: collide_against_search calls it with (probe, search+0x38) and it is
# a pass over a cell grid (0x003b4d50 looks up the cell from grid +0x40 origin, +0x3c cell size, +0x34
# width, +0x50 count; also 0x003b4ef0, 0x003b4de0, 0x003b4fe0, the recursive 0x001e8e00 and 0x00218f50),
# then fires the probe's listeners vt +8 and its hit bit / +0x10c callback. The plane's search+0x38 is
# 0, so it does nothing in the ride. Kind 8 (a finite plane, 0x003dc030) keeps the nearest by fraction
# below +0xb0 and copies point/normal to +0x30/+0x20. Neither is ported.

# S49 — the sweep probe's grid pass, kind 8 and smoothed normals: none is reachable on this disc, so
# none is ported; tests/sweep_probe_reach.rs re-proves it from the disc and the save states.
# The grid: a search's +0x38 is zeroed by search_init and written only twice, by the physics-object
# init 0x001597b0 (its own search at +0x11c, gated on +0x124) and by 0x001a5210, both copying the
# level's +0x42c. That is written only by terrain_grid_load, called from the level load 0x001a2c30
# when the level's .scn has a [SoultreeTerrain] section with a File (".tcg" put on its name). No .scn
# on the disc has that section and no archive holds a .tcg, so +0x42c stays 0 and every search's +0x38
# with it: sweep_probe_grid_pass (and the ray probe's +0xb0 0x001ebd20, and 0x001f1670) never runs.
# The grid itself (0x003b4470 ctor, 0xa0 bytes) is built nowhere else. The plane's vtable is at
# plane+0x48, so the init's +0x11c/+0x124/+0x12c are plane+0x164/+0x16c/+0x174; in biplane.p2m2's state
# +0x16c is the collision object's own nearby search, which search_init built with +0x38 = 0.
# Kind 8: finite_plane_init (vtable +0x38 finite_plane_class, +0x68 = 8, planes +0x120 x 0xd0, count
# +0x124) is called only by collision_file_load, for a .col whose type word (+12, after 1234, "col",
# version 1|2) is 8. Of the disc's 4220 .col files none is: 173 v1 type 0, 235 v1 type 2, 3198 v2 type
# 0, 21 v2 type 1, 593 v2 type 2. (Its in-memory path reads the type at buffer+4 of the same file.)
# finite_plane_sweep is the segment test sphere_collide_pair runs for kind 8; 0x00150190 is its other
# caller. The 0x003dc490.. functions nearby are an unrelated sound emitter class (vtable 0x0045eb40).
# Smoothed normals: probe_smooth_flag is stored only by sphere_probe_pair (from +0x164) and
# ray_probe_pair (from +0x174) — a scan of every store in the code for its low half, and for $gp+0xae0.
# A sweep probe's +0x164 is zeroed by its ctor 0x001e80f0; none of its 17 construction sites and none
# of its class slots writes it again, and the 90 raw +0x164 stores in the code all belong to other
# classes (fonts' glyph state, quads at +0x160, the collidable class of 0x001ea830, $sp slots).
# In all six save states on disk every sweep probe's +0x164 (64 / 91) and every ray probe's +0x174
# (47 / 54) is 0, as is the flag, the level's grid, and no finite plane exists.
search_init boot 0x002188d0 (search) zeroes it; +0x38 the grid = 0
sweep_probe_grid_pass boot 0x001e93e0 the sweep probe's class slot +0xb0 (= sphere_probe_prepare above)
terrain_grid_load boot 0x001a33e0 (level, path, position) the .tcg into level+0x42c, or 0
level_object boot 0x0048a568 the level; its +0x42c is the terrain grid every search copies
sweep_probe_class boot 0x00451910 the sweep probe's class vtable, at a collidable's +0x38
ray_probe_class boot 0x00451a90 the ray probe's class vtable, at a collidable's +0x38
finite_plane_class boot 0x0045e9d0 the finite plane collidable's class vtable (kind 8)
finite_plane_init boot 0x003db9e0 (collidable) kind 8
collision_file_load boot 0x0011b230 (owner, name, ...) loads a .col by its type word; type 8 makes a finite plane
finite_plane_sweep boot 0x003dc030 kind 8's segment test: nearest fraction below +0xb0, point/normal
probe_smooth_flag boot 0x00468250 set from the probe's +0x164 (sweep) or +0x174 (ray) per pair test
# S46 — the ray probe's narrow phase (ray_collide_pair against a mesh or a group), ported as ray_probe.
# The result record (probe +0x6c): +0 / +0x10 the nearest contact's segment, +0x20 its normal, +0x30
# its point, +0x40 push = low + high, +0x50 low, +0x60 high, +0x70 the pair's nearest point, +0x80 its
# fraction, +0x84 the last group part struck, +0x88 capacity, +0x8c count, +0x90 rays (two quads each;
# 0 = no list), +0x94 normals, +0x98 points, +0x9c objects, +0xa0 placed words, +0xa8 fractions,
# +0xac segment words, +0xb0 nearest fraction, +0xb4 flags (1 placed, 2 smooth), +0xb8 one per ray.
collision_record_make_current boot 0x0011c110 (record) the global the record writers use = record
current_collision_record boot 0x00468258 the record ray_collide_pair and the leaf step write into
ray_tree_pair boot 0x00109130 (probe root, mesh root, &{probe nodes, segments}, &{mesh nodes, facets},
#                              probe T, mesh T, 1, mesh descriptor; stack: probe +0x178) the frames
#                              (mesh to probe behind 0x0041da30, its inverse 0x0041da40, magnitudes
#                              0x0041da50) and the tree globals 0x00468a70..0x00468a88, then descends
ray_tree_descend boot 0x00108980 (probe word, mesh word) both leaves: the leaf/leaf; one: the step then
#                              the node's two children (both tried); else the boxes, then the side
#                              whose key is larger (the mesh's on <=) opens
ray_box_box boot 0x00106000 (a centre, a half, b centre, b half) b through 0x0041da40 into a's frame:
#                              the fifteen axes, a's x z y first, each cross passed early on a's share
ray_leaf_step boot 0x001079d0 (probe word, mesh word) a segment against a mesh node (ray_segment_box),
#                              or a probe node against a triangle (thirteen axes, in the probe's frame)
ray_segment_box boot 0x00106820 (centre, half, from, to, frame) the segment's midpoint and half-span
ray_segment_triangle_pair boot 0x00108340 (segment word, facet word) the segment into the mesh's frame,
#                              then ray_segment_triangle; the record's contact, low/high, nearest
ray_segment_triangle boot 0x0010b980 the segment test; with smooth normals off it is collide::test
ray_group_gate boot 0x001eb340 (probe, other) the probe's node 0 box (or its first segment) against a
#                              group's +0x120 / +0x130 box through ray_group_boxes, or a mesh's node 0
ray_group_boxes boot 0x00109330 (other centre, other half, probe centre, probe half, other, probe) the
#                              frame probe to other+0x1a0 into 0x0041da40, then ray_box_box

# P30, the stick the backflip reads. For the human rider, record P = bike + 0x20.
# The pad listener pushes one sample per call into a 32-slot ring on P: head P+0xa0, time
# P+0xa4+4*slot (Timer0 ms x 0.001, the wall clock), axes P+0x124+0x10*slot (LX, LY with the d-pad as
# -/+1, RX, RY). The dispatcher runs after the pad update, after the sim, and from the DMA waits each
# time one passes 4 ms: ~5 samples a frame in the save states, not a constant.
# player_input_dispatch then writes each axis's flick speed to P+0x638+0x18*i (LY's is P+0x650): the
# ring scanned oldest to newest, a sample past +-0.8 remembered, the next past 0.8 on the other side
# giving (v - v0) / (t - t0) when t0 > 0, the largest magnitude kept, the memory cleared either way.
# pad_to_gesture copies P+0x650 to bike+0x630, the d-pad flags to +0x620/+0x624, the brake lever to
# +0x640. The limiter squares +0x628/+0x62c in place (x*|x|) for the human (P+0x34 == 1), then walks
# the lean pair +0x670/+0x674 toward them at XDPad/YDPadRateLimit (+0x668/+0x66c) on an axis whose
# d-pad flag is set, else X/YAxisRateLimit (+0x660/+0x664): now + min(max(want-now, -r*dt), r*dt);
# +0x670 is zeroed while +0x610 is clear. Preload and backflip read +0x62c and +0x630 unlimited.
pad_ring_listener boot 0x0025d240 (listener, phase) one timed sample of the four axes into P's ring
pad_ring_register boot 0x0025d810 puts pad_ring_listener on the app's listener list
listener_dispatch boot 0x001321d0 (phase) calls every registered listener; the tick and the DMA waits call it
bike_input_limiter boot 0x00251a50 (dt in f12, bike) the bike's vt+0xec: squares the stick, walks the lean pair

# S45 — the broad phase (collision_search_gather, gather_resort, the world tree query, above),
# ported as broad_phase::{gather, resort, Query} with probe_collide_world. A space node is
# (+0 next, +4 table); a table has its static structure at +0x34, its moving one at +0x38, and
# sub-tables at +0x44 (count +0x48). A structure's vtable is at +8, its flags at +0x14 (bit0 a query
# is open, bit1 it reports the box its cells cover).
world_tree_global_table boot 0x0048aac0 the table every global query ends with (0 in the save state)
world_tree_stamp boot 0x0045f804 u16 (gp-32620): bumped per quadtree query; a collidable's +0 u16
#                              holds the stamp it was last handed out under
world_tree_current boot 0x0048a528 the quadtree last begun or walked
world_tree_cell_size boot 0x0048a530 world units per quadtree cell, for the box begin reports
world_manager boot 0x0046a5f8 word: the world manager (+0x94 model table, +0xd4 placed-group cell size)
quadtree_begin boot 0x00196e90 quadtree vt (0x00451060) +0x30 (tree, query, half, lo, hi): origin
#                              +0x30/+0x38, extent +0x48/+0x4c, cell +0x58, scale +0x5c, root +0x94,
#                              pool +0x9c, list +0xa4/+0xa8, slot +0xac, chunk +0xb0, +0x898 busy,
#                              cells +0x8a8..+0x8b4
quadtree_gather boot 0x00197160 (node, list, x0, z0, x1, z1, ox, oz, size, least) the nodes meeting
#                              the cells, prepended; node +0 children array, +4 chunk (+0 next, +4..)
quadtree_gather_all boot 0x00197370 (node, list) a whole subtree, post-order
quadtree_child boot 0x00197560 (node, i) -> child i
quadtree_pool_take boot 0x001974f0 one list link from the pool
quadtree_next boot 0x00197580 quadtree vt +0x38
quadtree_restart boot 0x00197d70 quadtree vt +0x3c
quadtree_end boot 0x00197d90 quadtree vt +0x40
block_list_begin boot 0x001ab7f0 moving-object list vt (0x00450220) +0x30: block size +0x30, blocks
#                              +0x34, used in the last +0x38, array +0x3c, where +0x44/+0x48
block_list_next boot 0x001ab850 block list vt +0x38: full blocks stamped, the last block not
block_list_restart boot 0x001ab9c0 block list vt +0x3c
block_list_end boot 0x001ab9f0 block list vt +0x40
point_distance boot 0x0011c170 (reach f12, probe, point) ground-plane distance less both reaches, >= 0
pair_distance boot 0x0011c1b0 (a, b) distance less both reaches, >= 0
plane_refresh_search boot 0x0015b210 the plane collision owner's (plane+0x50) vt +0x110 off its vptr at
#                              +0x38 (vtable 0x0045b2ac; S96's +0x108 was off +0x10): marks its probes
#                              moved, then collision_search_gather(+0x11c = plane+0x16c, the ray probe
#                              +0x130, 3) -- what refills the plane's nearby search; callers in S99's block

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# R9 — the two-player game, read statically from the decompilation and the disc: nothing here was run
# (no two-player state exists; plan/HUMAN.md asks for one). Journal:
# research/journal/2026-10-20-r9-r9-two-players. Feeds S37, V22 and P20.
# Pointers: app = *(0x0041f770). app+0x3d4 settings: +0 NumberOfRacers, +8 the viewport-mode object
# (+4 split mode 0 single / 1 horizontal / 2 vertical, +0x10 a flag forcing a 16:9 aspect), +0xc the
# event (+0 league, +4 activity type, +0x10c bike class (R7), +0x114 human players, +0x116 (0: the AI made
# by difficulty; else spawn_racers takes them from a list off app+0x3f4, not read), +0x117 AI difficulty, +0x134+i*4 player i's camera choice), +0x10 the per-player profiles.
# app+0x3cc the scene, app+0x104 the input manager, app+0x108 the terrain query.
#
# The display: two globals, both set by display_mode_set.
display_aspect              boot 0x004211a0 word: 0 None, 1 1x1, 2 4x3, 3 16x9 (names at 0x00420af0)
display_video_mode          boot 0x00421198 word: 0 None, 1 480i, 2 480p, 3 480p_Widescreen, 4 720p,
#                                           5 1080i, 6 PAL, 7 PAL60 (names at 0x00420ab0)
display_mode_set            boot 0x00312410 (mode, params): modes 1/2/6/7 -> aspect 2, 3/4/5 -> 3,
#                                           params[1] overrides; sets the frame buffer (640x448 at 480i)
#                                           and the aspect ratios 0x004e2224/28/2c (1:1, 4:3, 16:9).
#                                           Callers 0x0016a280 (probes 1, 2, 7, 6) and 0x00194f70 (1<->2)
#
# The camera presets. cpresets.ini is appstart\cpresets.ini in every Tracks\<track>_2.res (and an
# identical copy in _ss_2.res): 11 presets x 5 layouts. Its own header names them, and camera_load_presets
# (above, S7) picks by the camera's split mode cam+0x174 and the display aspect:
#   aspect != 3:  mode 0 Preset%d (1 player) · 1 Preset%dh (2P horizontal) · 2 Preset%dv (2P vertical)
#                 · else Preset%dq
#   aspect == 3:  mode 0 and 1 Preset%dw (1P wide) · 2 Preset%dx (2P vertical wide) · else Preset%dz
#   strings 0x0042d3b0 / 0x0042d3c0 h / 0x0042d3d0 v / 0x0042d3e0 q / 0x0042d380 w / 0x0042d390 x /
#   0x0042d3a0 z. q and z (a four-way split, by the pattern) are in no file on the disc, and a missing
#   section is skipped, leaving the record as it was; nothing sets a mode above 2. So: w and x are
#   16:9, not quarters, and a 16:9 horizontal split rides the 1P-wide presets.
#   Preset1 AngleX 65 FollowingDistance 13 MinFollowingDistance 5 FOV 85; Preset1h AngleX 30 Following 16
#   MinFollowing 7 FOV 80 LookAhead 15 LowSpeedAngleXScaleFactor 0.75; Preset1v AngleX 65 Following 13
#   MinFollowing 7 FOV 60 CameraSwing 2.5 LookSwing 2.0; Preset1w and Preset1x read as Preset1.
mxgame_configure            boot 0x00203260 the boot config: key ViewportMode (0x0042da48) "Horizontal"
#                                           -> players >= 2, event+0x114 = 2, viewport object +4 = 1;
#                                           "Vertical" the same with +4 = 2
race_camera_set_split       boot 0x0021f6e0 (cam, mode): cam+0x174 = mode, reloads the presets, re-sets
#                                           the current one. The base class's 0x001fc8d0, vtable +0xe4
player_set_split            boot 0x00200960 (player, mode): player+0x130 = mode, then the race camera's
#                                           race_camera_set_split
#
# The split itself.
viewport_build              boot 0x0020a7e0 (game): one viewport (0x360 bytes, ctor 0x00168ac0) per
#                                           human player, at game+0x34+i*4; player i's camera from
#                                           event+0x134+i*4 (1 the follow camera +0x54, else +0x5c);
#                                           clears bit 3 of app+0x325; ends in viewport_arrange
player_view_build           boot 0x0020a360 (game, i): the per-player view (0x158 bytes, ctor
#                                           0x001feda0, init 0x001feea0) at game+0x64+i, its cameras at
#                                           game+0x3c+i*0x14 (five slots)
viewport_arrange            boot 0x0020aaa0 (game): mode = game+0x78 (copied from the viewport object
#                                           by the world loader). Every mode calls player_set_split per
#                                           player and sets each view's aspect through vtable +200
#                                           (0x004e2220[aspect], or 16:9 with the wide flag or aspect 3).
#                                           Rectangles by viewport_set_rect, w x h the frame buffer:
#                                             0 one view (0, 0, w, h)
#                                             1 horizontal: (0, 0, w, h/2) above (0, h/2, w, h/2)
#                                             2 vertical:   (0, 0, w/2, h) beside (w/2, 0, w/2, h)
#                                           Fog: with two views and league != 0 the far distance is the
#                                           scene's MaximumFogDistance x 0.61 (supercross, league 0, keeps
#                                           it); fog record scene+0x3b8 (+0x18 min, +0x1c max). Freestyle
#                                           (activity 5) sets the near clip to 2.4 (0x00112a80)
viewport_set_rect           boot 0x00113b40 (view, x, y, w, h) plus the centring offsets 0x004e2208 /
#                                           0x004e2210
fog_set                     boot 0x0012f940 (far f12, near f13, 64.0 f14, fog) -> fog+0x6c far, +0x70 near,
#                                           +0x74 the 64, then fog_apply; the fog object is scene+0x420
fog_new                     boot 0x0012f4d0 (Visibility, Haziness, Max, Min, 64.0, fog, colour): the fog
#                                           object's constructor, from the scene's fog record scene+0x3b8
#                                           (+0x10 Visibility, +0x14 Haziness, +0x18 Min, +0x1c Max)
fog_apply                   boot 0x0012f6a0 (vis, haz, blend time, fog, colour): both fractions clamped
#                                           0..1 to +0x3c/+0x40; end +0x38 = near + vis*(far-near), start
#                                           +0x34 = (1-haz)*(end-64). A blend time > 1e-7 only stores the
#                                           target (+0x50..+0x5c) for fog_tick to ease towards
fog_tick                    boot 0x0012f970 (fog, dt): eases colour and both fractions to the target
fog_to_view                 boot 0x0012fb00 (fog): the view's far clip (0x00112aa0) = end, then the
#                                           display object's vtable +0x3c (renderer_set_fog) with start/end
renderer_set_fog            boot 0x00179ea0 (display, start, end): +0x48 start, +0x4c end, +0x7c
#                                           255/(start-end) — linear, FOG 255 (clear) at the start, 0 (all
#                                           fog colour) at the end. A model wholly nearer than the start is
#                                           drawn without fog (display+0x48 against distance+radius)
scene_fog_read              boot 0x001a8a50 (record, ini): [Fog] ColorRGB, Visibility (default 1),
#                                           Haziness (0.5), MinimumFogDistance (64), MaximumFogDistance
#                                           (768), each through strtod + double_to_float (truncating)
#
# short_viewport (crates/game-sim camera::Switches): the race camera's aim, 0x0021f1bc..0x0021f2b0.
# Skipped when aspect == 3, or when *(app+0x3cc)+0x78 != 0 (meaning not found); otherwise if the view's
# pixel height over width (viewport+0x18c / +0x188) is under 0.55, pitch -= 0.33 x the preset's AngleX.
# At 640x448 only the horizontal split's 640x224 (0.35) is under it: full screen is 0.7, the vertical
# split's 320x448 is 1.4. So it is the 4:3 horizontal split's and nothing else's.
#
# What two views cut back.
world_load                  boot 0x00205c40 two views (mode != 0) in a free area (league 2) loads the
#                                           track as "%s_SS" (0x0042def0): Tracks\freeNN_ss.res + _ss_2.res.
#                                           free06_ss against free06: no rut meshes (56) or rut texture,
#                                           no crow and its animation, no loop_01.vue, no free06_detail.pst,
#                                           its own terrain .trn and a smaller .scn (99,368 bytes against
#                                           126,286, so fewer props). Supercross and nationals have no _ss
shadow_ranges               boot 0x00208700 players < 2: shadow_set_range(100, 40); >= 2: (65, 10)
shadow_set_range            boot 0x00250c60 (dist, fade): stores dist^2 and (dist - fade)^2 — 1P draws
#                                           shadows to 100 ft, fading from 60; 2P to 65, fading from 55.
#                                           shadows.res, one terrainShadowReceiver%d per racer, up to 8
# The vehicle models' "_ss" skins (biplane_ss, Kiowa_ss, buggy_ss, ...) are picked by vehicle_factory's
# skin index (R8, above); whether two views are what picks them was not read.
#
# Pads and riders.
spawn_racers                boot 0x00207b20 (world, humans): humans first (player 0x6c8 bytes, ctor
#                                           0x00369b00, vtable 0x0045bda0), each init'ed through vtable
#                                           +0xb8 with kind 1 and pad index i; AI after with pad -1,
#                                           NumberOfRacers - humans of them, their skill band from
#                                           event+0x117. player+0x6b0 racer index, +0x6b4 the profile
#                                           settings+0x10[i]
player_init                 boot 0x0025d810 (player, kind, a2, pad, f12 0.3) (Player.cpp; vtable +0xb8
#                                           is 0x00369bb0, a jump to it): +0x34 = kind; pad >= 0 binds
#                                           input device i: +0x68 = input_mgr+0xc+i*4, +0x6c = +0x14+i*4,
#                                           and builds the rumble objects. So human i is pad i, fixed
# freeride_racer_count (S14, 0x002b0a20): NumberOfRacers = 4 with one player, else the player count —
# a two-player freeride has no AI riders. A race keeps its AI.
scene_info_read             boot 0x001a6f50 (out, file) [SceneInfo] DefaultStartPosition /
#                                           DefaultStartDirection into out, out+0x10 (one per scene;
#                                           sx01: 101.49,90.12,458.48 facing +z). Where the second rider
#                                           is put relative to it, or on a gate, was not found
scene_gates_read            boot 0x002af2e0 the scene's Funnel_Up / Funnel_Down / DropGate / LowerGate /
#                                           RaiseGate sections into scene+0xb0/+0xac/+0xb4/+0xb8/+0xbc;
#                                           no per-lane start positions in it
#
# The HUD. ui_load (0x0022a5a0) reads a layer's Viewport key as a mask: viewport_1 1, _2 2, _3 4, _4 8,
# all 0xf, last/full 0x10 (the default). No in-game layer on the disc sets it: each player's HUD is a
# layer of its own in full-screen coordinates. Layout sections are "%s %s %s Layouts" (language, video
# mode, aspect). mxingame.lod groups: SinglePlayer, Ghost, MultiplayerVertical, MultiplayerHorizontal,
# Freestyle*, FastLapAttack*. Per player (…1 and …2): InfoTextOverlay; RacingOverlays (Time, Lead, Lap,
# Position, CountdownBoard, OffTrackCountdown/Warning, Best Lap, WhiteFlag, CheckerFlag);
# StuntScoreOverlays (Score, Stunt, Stunt Combo); Speedometer (PreloadMeter, analog, digital); in

# (Vertical/Horizontal), Alert, DebugOverlays, SplitScreenSeparator (splitscreenseparator.lyt, X 629
# Y 27), HitLights (Player1..4HitLight), FreestyleFullScreenInfoDialog. Two-player groups drop
# RaceLeagueFinalStandingsMenu, Demo and the ghost layers. Strings: RacingOverlaysHorizontal%d
# 0x004354b0, RacingOverlaysVertical%d 0x00435500, Player%dHitLight 0x004403a0.

# --- V28: the rest of the riding HUD, read for crates/openmx/src/overlay.rs (score bar, preload meter).
#     Both are Flash movies (Overlay_1p_Score.fsh, ingame_preloadmeter.fsh) the port has no player

speedo_preload_var          boot 0x00436f78 string "preloadMeter": speedo_update binds the meter movie's

# --- V53: what the preload meter is fed. crates/openmx/src/overlay.rs PreloadMeter ---
speedo_preload_update       boot 0x002abe10 called first thing in speedo_needle_update, every frame.
#                                           iface = speedo+0x3c (the rider's MXUIInterface component,
#                                           rider+0xfd0; its pointers bound by boot 0x0027c220):
#                                           +0x24 -> rider+0x4a0 preload ready, +0x28 -> +0x4a4 firing,
#                                           +0x30 -> +0x868 (copy of +0x748, the wreck reason),
#                                           +0x34 -> +0x494 preload force y, +0x38 -> +0x4c4 MaxPreload.
#                                           Unlatched (speedo+0x50 == 0): value = ready ? 0 : -1. Firing
#                                           and unlatched: latch; n = cvt.w.s(0.5 + 20 * (force / max)),
#                                           n < 2 -> 1; value = n unless the wreck reason is set (then
#                                           -1, unlatched). Each value change pokes the movie (vtable
#                                           +0xbc of speedo+0x48's +0x6c)
speedo_anim_done            boot 0x002abfc0 the movie's event: "AnimDone" (0x00436f88, case-blind
#                                           compare boot 0x00410c50) with its arg's +0xe == 1 clears
#                                           the latch
# Captured (context/capture/v53/, recording2 played back, speedo 0x01b0a2c0 / rider 0x00da5050 in its
# state): -1 riding or in the air, 0 on a lip, 10/7/5/3 for shoves of 285.7/222.2/148.9/104.1 of 600,
# each latched 14 + n ticks. The movie (PCSX2 video capture of the same playback) steps once a tick:
# rises two levels a tick to n, holds n 15 ticks, falls through the odd levels below n, empty on the
# 14+n'th tick (AnimDone). Lit-empty capsule ~88,44,10; charge ~206,109,0; -1 is the dark capsule
speedo_show_a               boot 0x002ac190 speedometer part visibility setters (three, by flag); which

preload_tip_popup           boot 0x0029aad0 the "Preload Movie" / "Clutch Movie" tip popup (vtable
#                                           0x00455d30): the tutorial tip, not the meter
#
# Two riders touching. Nothing two-player about it: it is the same contact clause S37 ported
# (air::hit_wreck), and AI riders meet it too. A record's sweep writes each actor it hits twice:
collision_record_collide    boot 0x002708a0 (above, S37) after the terrain rays it runs the ray object's
#                                           actor query (0x0011a150, or 0x0011a9d0 with record+0x10c set)
#                                           and walks its hits (+0x6c: count +0x8c, rays +0x90, normals
#                                           +0x94, points +0x98, hit +0x9c whose +0x80 is the other
#                                           actor's record): an own entry (kind 0, other = theirs) and,
#                                           when it is an actor, a mirrored one (kind 1, other = ours)
#                                           into their record; both get +0x40/+0x44/+0x4c = 1
collision_sphere_collide    boot 0x00272ce0 the same two writes for a sphere-bodied record (vtable
#                                           0x004549ec), from the rigid body at +0x210
# The wreck test then reads only the own entries: the head ray's hit on a rider whose chassis is more
# than JoustWreckHeight (2.5 on race250p/race250a/pro250p) above ours is reason 7 for us, thrown with
# their velocity — the rider underneath goes down, the one on top rides on. The mirrored entry only
# tells the other bike it was touched.
# P34, the camera's target block. race_camera_update's actor (cam+0x8c) is not the vehicle: it is a
# component at MotocrossVehicle (the type-2 rider) + 0xdd0, built by actor_class_descriptor_ctor in
# the type-2 ctor 0x00279ed0. Its fields, as rider offsets: +0xde0 point (actor+0x10), +0xe00
# forward (+0x30), +0xe10 up (+0x40), +0xe20 velocity (+0x50), +0xe54 lean (+0x84), +0xe80 flag
# (+0xb0), +0xe84 speed (+0xb4).
vehicle_camera_target boot 0x0027c580 (rider) fills the block once a frame, gated on
#                                     (rider+8) & (rider+0xdd4): point = rider+0x820 (the seat),
#                                     rows = orientation_axes (0x001b24d0) of the node rider+0x18 (the
#                                     chassis transform, P32), vel = rider+0x840, lean = rider+0x854,
#                                     speed = max.s(rider+0x860, 0) (+0x860 = *(rider+0x7a4)+0x34), and
#                                     flag = sltu/xori 1 of rider+0x93c, i.e. on_ground == 0: AIRBORNE.
#                                     +0x93c is the snapshot of +0x574 (either wheel touches, V9). On
#                                     the first airborne frame it also keeps the takeoff position and
#                                     velocity at +0xe60/+0xe70 (no reader looked for)
# Checked in RAM: first_recording's state (bike on the ground) +0x93c = 1, +0xe80 = 0; recording2's
# biplane state (plane on its wheels) +0xe80 = 0. Captured through recording2's playback, frame_counter
# 2375..3443 (1070 rows): +0xe80 == !(+0x574) on every row; through both backflips (2500..2610,
# 3284..3430) render_camera's view stays within 1.5 deg of the velocity while 180 deg off the forward
# row, and the eye stays 13.3 / 14.6 ft or more behind the bike.
